13170: void acc_radar_engine_a121_init_interrupts(acc_sensor_id_t sensor_id)
13171: {
13172: 	acc_sensor_protocol_a121_init_interrupts(sensor_id);
13175: {
13176: 	// Registers do not retain their values in hibernation.
